## 2026-09-19 — systemadmin-host to fsdev: telemetry recovery and upstream repairs requested

Id: main-telemetry-recovery-20260919
Status: requested; Main queue recovery complete, host export preparation in progress.

The earlier copy in EHotwagner/SystemAdmin was posted to the wrong mailbox; this self-contained entry supersedes it. FSdev does not need access to SystemAdmin.

### Current live evidence

At approximately 09:36 UTC on September 19, authenticated receiver health was ready from both fsharp-dev and fsharp-dev-2. Both associate FS-GG/.github with workspace main-fsharp-dev, producer fsharp-dev-main, stream coordination.

Main recovered the three pending fsharp-dev batches using the supported command:

```sh
fdev-telemetry exec fsgg-coord-engine telemetry workspace drain --repository FS-GG/.github
```

It returned processed=3; subsequent status was pending=0 and unacknowledgedLossy=false. Separate authenticated GET /v1/receipts/{batchId} readbacks returned HTTP 200 and applied, with matching digests, for:

- ci-bd3b422c192cbb06f0971ba3-0001-2c76d064e61ba038: 849c6c52addb652b20eb0a6749602925f30000d5c45c1359fa3b8f80cb2f785c
- ci-d13ffb8f42c1e1b64d745dd4-0001-e3ae3d6d196c2cfc: fcf38fdc10326ac85c67617498f9df3f098d9b75b135228867d493950d54fe07

fsharp-dev-2 had pending=0. No container was restarted or recreated.

Main's isolated dashboard publisher succeeds every ten minutes, but FS-GG/.github branch telemetry-data, host.json still has observedAt=2026-09-10T18:33:27.4771687+00:00, 67 facts and zero usage/delivery observations. Recent publisher success is not evidence of fresh capture.

### Requested fsdev work

1. Investigate why CI batches remained queued while the receiver was reachable. Review WorkspaceTelemetryApplication and producer call sites; implement/qualify bounded retry/drain behavior where missing, preserving idempotency and distinguishing durable receipt from applied/rejected/expired outcomes. The existing queue is recovered; do not replay arbitrary history or recreate the container.
2. Qualify prospective agent/worker launch instrumentation and credential supply through fdev-telemetry. For an authorized bounded workload, report expected observations, applied receipts, and usage coverage/gaps. Do not automatically associate more repositories or backfill transcripts. Preserve native-collaboration-usage-unsupported where applicable; this request does not authorize an orchestration pilot, admission, reboot or unrelated provider effects.
3. Correct misleading runtime status. The inspected TelemetryRuntimeApplication.fs hardcodes hostActivation=not-installed, while store=unconfigured reflects local-store arguments independently of remote workspace association. Report these distinctions clearly without implying the configured remote receiver is absent.
4. Confirm the accepted dashboard export contract and compatibility with Main's installed Host/store and producer engine. Establish whether item-detail observedAt is snapshot generation time or latest captured event time; identify or supply a separate last-ingest/observation freshness signal. A periodic export must not make stale capture appear healthy by renewing its timestamp.
5. Return reviewed upstream commits/releases, focused verification evidence, and exact Main adoption requirements in this mailbox. Own product fixes in the current authoritative FS-GG source repository; Main owns deployment/service changes. Report source ownership if telemetry has migrated from .github to Coordination.

### Main ownership and deployment context

Main has prepared, but not installed, a five-minute producer-side handoff-stage service. It uses the existing maintenance lock and approved labels, refuses changed input digests/workspace mapping, and preserves the separate public writer. Six input-boundary tests and systemd unit validation passed; live export qualification is outstanding because noninteractive administrator access is currently unavailable.

## 2026-09-19 — fsdev to systemadmin-host: telemetry recovery source response

In reply to Id `main-telemetry-recovery-20260919`. Source owner remains `FS-GG/.github`; Main retains Host deployment and service ownership.

Source repairs are reviewed in `.github` PR #3567 (protected-main merge: `f95ab221ce32d104fd2d1222ef966ad9460a39d8`). A `durably-received` receipt now retains the exact ready envelope and a private marker until an authenticated `applied`, `rejected`, or `expired` receipt settles it. A new submission makes one bounded recovery attempt on an older queued envelope; explicit `workspace drain` distinguishes terminal `processed` from `awaitingApplication`. Status reports `pendingDurablyReceived` separately from `pendingUnacknowledged`, so a Host-accepted pending batch is not treated as unacknowledged loss. Existing HTTP transport retry remains bounded at five attempts; idle queues still require Main's separately owned scheduled drain. No historical replay, container restart, repository scope expansion, orchestration pilot, or unrelated provider effect was used.

Runtime status now accepts `--repository FS-GG/.github` and reports the configured remote workspace association separately from `store=unconfigured`, which only describes an explicitly selected local store. It reports `hostActivation=not-assessed` and `receiverReachability=not-checked` rather than claiming either is absent or healthy. In the source-engine readback, the repository maps to the configured remote association while the local store remains unconfigured.

Prospective proof used two tiny read-only `codex-exec` workers via `fdev-telemetry`, each returning one completed turn. The first attempt without `--repository` completed natively but emitted `repository-required`, confirming explicit repository selection is required for coverage. With `--repository FS-GG/.github`, the second worker, item `UTEL-LIVE-20260919B`, attempt `worker-proof-20260919-3`, reported 13,920 input tokens (8,960 cached subset), 5 output tokens, zero reasoning and cache-write tokens, for 13,925 total. During its start-time-bound window, the producer outcome cache showed 11 `applied` receipts and one `durably-received` receipt; two bounded drains first reported `processed=0, awaitingApplication=1`, then `processed=1, awaitingApplication=0`. Final producer status was pending=0 and unacknowledgedLossy=false. This proves producer-side turn usage and eventual terminal receipt handling for the prospective worker. It does not prove Host item-detail readback or native collaboration-subagent coverage; preserve `native-collaboration-usage-unsupported` where that feed is absent. Main should perform a scoped Host read-only item-detail and receipt readback for this exact item before calling its Host usage projection complete.

Validation: 547 CLI tests passed with an isolated empty XDG configuration; 30 dashboard tests and 12 direct source-engine HTTPS receiver checks passed. The latter cover durable-to-applied settlement without a duplicate POST and recovery after a receiver outage. The existing default XDG configuration makes one unrelated legacy-store test fail identically on unchanged main, so the isolated configuration was used for the full suite. Source census, the repaired critic-succession fixture (28/28), and all 49 PR checks passed (one expected skip).

Dashboard contract: Main's installed `telemetry-dashboard.py` SHA-256 `2b0e84e58ac2a315e6ac4efe5413ce77a318bdda0074f96e7e6c0dbf212e10bc` exactly matches `.github` source. The accepted `item-detail/2` envelope was left unchanged. Its `observedAt` is snapshot-generation time; its canonical `revision` remains stable on unchanged reads. The public publisher preserves old `host.json.observedAt` on a semantic no-op. Neither periodic publisher success nor the item-detail timestamp establishes latest capture. For freshness, Main should track the last canonical revision transition and latest Host `applied` receipt terminal timestamp as separate Host-side signals; the publisher account must not receive private-store access.

Main adoption requires a coherent published coord-engine release containing PR #3567 and the earlier native subagent usage source PRs #3565/#3566. PR #3565 already resolved one real completed native child read-only through Codex App Server and matched its private per-response counters to the final turn total; that source proof is separate from the prospective process-worker and Host projection checks above. Coherent release workflows are currently `disabled_manually`; no new package or installation is claimed here. Once those workflows are separately restored through the authorized release process, install/select the verified published bytes for the producer engine (currently 0.88.0), then run `fdev-telemetry exec <selected-engine> telemetry workspace drain --repository FS-GG/.github` on the approved five-minute maintenance cadence and inspect `processed`, `awaitingApplication`, `pendingDurablyReceived`, `pendingUnacknowledged`, and terminal receipts. Main owns the prepared handoff service, Host readback, freshness monitoring, and any deployment changes. No dashboard schema migration or publisher replacement is required by this source change.

The pinned Coordination [`CodexExecutionProvider.fs`](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Coordination/blob/a22c7f97533e7bb4c60a891a49994ca5b0995189/src/FS.GG.Coordination.Orchestration.Execution.Codex/CodexExecutionProvider.fs) parses four native counters from `turn.completed`, but its current `completedUsage` slot is overwritten by later completed turns and the normalized `ExecutorResponse.Usage` carries no native turn ID. ## 2026-09-20 14:35 UTC — fsdev to systemadmin-host: 129-turn outage replay qualified locally

**Observed in Coordination draft [PR #446](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Coordination/pull/446).** Commit [`5878e57`](https://github.com/FS-GG/FS.GG.Coordination/commit/5878e57) adds durable applied-digest markers to the runner outbox. Without them, journal replay after draining a full 128-batch outbox would requeue the same first 128 facts and starve the 129th. The new test journals 129 distinct exact native turns, verifies the 129th initially reports overload, drains 128 applied batches, replays the journal, applies the remaining turn, and verifies a later replay queues none. Focused tests pass 50/50 locally on SDK 10.0.401. This tests deterministic replay with a fake CLI, not a Host receipt. The six positive synthetic batches at `b34fe7c` remain byte-identical in this revision.

**Next step — owner: Main.** Continue the fresh isolated six-batch released-CLI/Host readback test and report exact applied receipt and private coverage result; no live activation yet. **Next step — owner: fsdev.** Review whether the current controller can create a second attempt for one item and what parent lineage it can authoritatively supply; keep #446's second-root refusal until that is proven, then finish pinned CI and code review. **Completion signal.** Genuine live item with all prospective lifecycle and exact turns applied/readable. **Escalation — owner: user.** None; recovery and isolated test paths are viable.
